- **2026-10-03 · [payments I-1 / jobs I-2] The admin refund and chargeback emails are told while actionable, inside the KV window.** `refundOutcome` still re-derives its outputs on every settle (fix round 1, I-1), but builds `admin_refund_needed` only while Mollie's `amountRefunded` is below the amount to refund and the payment's first non-chargeback `refund_needed` event is under 6 days old (`EMAIL_WINDOW_MS`, now in `server/email-window.ts`): a dashboard refund days later, a chargeback or a replayed webhook no longer tells every admin again once the 7-day marker expired. `chargebackNotify` sends only for an amount that is not a reversal, within 6 days of when that amount was recorded: `charged_back_at` now holds when the stored amount last **rose** (it was the first time), so a second, higher chargeback is told too. Real retries all fall inside the window.
- **2026-10-03 · [jobs I-1] The email and reconciliation windows start when we applied the payment.** Mollie's `paidAt` stays in `paid_at` (the admin and the CSV show it), but `handlePaymentSettled` and the reconciliation's `PAID_AT` measure from the payment's first settling `sponsorship_event` (`payment_paid`, `revived`, `renewed`, `marked_paid_manually` with `data.paymentId`), falling back to `paid_at` (migrated rows). A bank transfer settled by the stale sweep 8 days after Mollie took the money now gets its render jobs and every email, and is re-sent by the reconciliation for 7 days from our settle. The correlated subquery goes through `payment_item` to `sponsorship_event` by `sponsorship_id` (the trail index); no migration. Correction to the task 4 entry and `settled.ts`: the events DLQ is reached after about 4 h (30+60+…+3600×3 s), not 10 h.
- **2026-10-03 · [payments M-1, M-2] The webhook's fan-out marker.** A throttled `already` now skips only `payment.settled`; `notify` (keyed) is always enqueued, so a chargeback arriving within the throttle is told. The marker (`fanout-marker.ts`, KV `mollie:fanout:<paymentId>`) moved to `@smog/sponsorships/server`, lives 5 minutes (the reconciliation's grace) instead of 1, and `sponsorships.paymentStatus` writes it after the fan-out of a payment it settled, so the webhook that follows the return page does not enqueue a second `payment.settled` (the sponsor emails no longer rest on the consumer's best-effort KV dedupe alone). It is written only after a successful enqueue, so a 503's retry and a poll whose enqueue was lost still fan out; the hourly reconciliation stays the backstop. Chosen over a `paid_at`-age rule, which would also have swallowed a webhook's own 503 retry.
- **2026-10-03 · [payments M-3] The compensation never fails a payment that exists at Mollie.** Its batch carries `failWhen("payment-at-mollie", mollie_id IS NOT NULL)`; on that guard the call answers the stored `checkout_url` (a concurrent repeat with the same `checkoutId` created it), so a retry that Mollie refused while the first create was in flight no longer frees the gestures of a payment the sponsor is about to pay.
- **2026-10-03 · [payments M-4] A logo key a sponsorship uses is refused.** Checkout and re-edit answer `INVALID_STATE logoInvalid` for a key any `sponsorship.logo_key` holds (`isLogoInUse`), before claiming or deleting anything (a re-edit keeping its own key is unchanged). Kept simple over a separate upload prefix: no key layout change, and `logo_key` has no index, which is fine at this table's size.
- **2026-10-03 · [payments M-5] Money already returned is not applied.** For an `open` or cancelled payment, Mollie `paid` with `amountRefunded + amountChargedBack >= amount` is handled like a mismatch: `refund_needed` (`reason: "mismatch"`, no new reason, so no admin copy change), items released, logged as an error. The chargeback email still goes out; a refund that covers it tells nobody (I-1).
- **2026-10-03 · [payments M-6] Another Mollie payment of ours.** When `metadata.paymentId` names our payment but ours stores another `mollie_id`, `settlePayment` answers the new outcome `duplicate`: nothing is applied, it is logged (`console.error` with both ids when paid, `warn` otherwise), and a paid one emails every admin `admin_refund_needed` (reason `double`, key `admin_refund_needed:<paymentId>:<mollieId>:<adminId>`) within 6 days of Mollie's `paidAt` and until Mollie's refund covers it. The webhook answers 200.
- **2026-10-03 · [payments M-7] A double click that loses its upload, and chargeback reversals.** When `claimLogo` finds no upload, the checkout re-reads its `checkoutId` and replays a payment a concurrent twin committed, instead of answering `logoInvalid` for a checkout that went through. A reversed chargeback (Mollie lowers `amountChargedBack`) lowers `charged_back_cents` to Mollie's value, guarded like the rise, logged `[sponsorships] Chargeback on payment … reversed`; no trail entry (`refund_needed` means money to return, and the event types are CHECKed) and no email. A partial reversal inside the 6-day window can make the next settle tell the admins the new, lower amount once (its key differs); accepted, since the amount is accurate.
- **2026-10-03 · [jobs M-1] An invalid email is dropped, not thrown.** `enqueueEmail`/`enqueueEvent` throw `InvalidMessageError` (schema or size); `enqueueOutputs` drops such an email with `[jobs] Dropped an invalid <template> message` in either mode and goes on, answering `false`. One admin whose address `z.email()` refuses no longer blocks the admins after them or dead-letters `payment.settled`; a queue failure still throws in `throw` mode.
- **2026-10-03 · [jobs M-4] The queue and R2 bindings are validated once per isolate.** `@smog/config/env/worker` gains `workerBindingsSchema`/`parseWorkerBindings` (`EMAIL_QUEUE`, `EVENTS_QUEUE`: `send`; `MEDIA`: the R2 object methods), and `siteEnv()` holds them as `bindings`. The crons, the events consumer, the webhook, the rpc context and the logo routes read them from there, so a missing binding fails at the first request instead of after the reminder sweep committed its state. This replaces ruling 8's `DirectEmailOutbox` fallback for a missing `EMAIL_QUEUE` in the site (every env binds it and the provisioning script creates it); the class stays for tests.
- **2026-10-03 · [jobs M-5] The renewal reminder promises the price then shown.** `email.transactional.renewalReminder.renew` says, in nl/en/fr, that a renewal is a new payment for one more year at the price then shown, the wording of the terms (`legal/*.ts`), instead of "the same amount as last year".
- **2026-10-03 · [jobs M-6] A partial purge logs what it did.** `runRetentionPurges` logs its counts and throws `RetentionPurgeError` (`counts`, `cause` = the first failure); `runRetentionPurge` logs `[sponsorships] The retention purge failed part way; done: {…}` with those and the logo counts before it rethrows, so a failing table never hides what the other purges deleted.
- **2026-10-03 · [jobs M-3] Docs and comments.** `docs/API.md` describes the stale sweep's kept-open payments, the full key list (`admin_chargeback`, the duplicate key) and the windows; `outbox.ts` (auth emails answer `503 EMAIL_NOT_SENT`) and `EmailRenderError` (retried into the DLQ) say what the code does.

## Phase 6 fix wave (UI)

- **2026-10-03 · The gesture CTA's decision table is one headless hook (review I-3).** `useSponsorCta(gesture, { linkShown })` in `@smog/sponsorships/client` returns the `SponsorCtaView` (`available` / `pending` / `sponsored` with name and end, or `null`) through the pure `sponsorCtaView`, which a bun test covers branch by branch: loading or failed with and without a credit, an answer without the gesture, available with checkout on, off and the link hidden, pending, sponsored with and without `sponsorName`, unavailable. The site and the app keep only their markup; the app passes `linkShown` (`sponsorLinkShown(platform, SPONSOR_LINK_IN_APP)`). The gesture type is structural (`{ id, sponsor }`), so the package takes no dependency on `@smog/gestures`.
- **2026-10-03 · One `INVALID_STATE` reason → copy map, in `@smog/sponsorships/schema` (review M-7).** `INVALID_STATE_REASON_KEYS` (`as const satisfies Record<InvalidStateReason, TranslationKey>`) sits next to `SPONSORSHIP_STATUS_LABEL_KEYS` (ruling 15), with `invalidStateReasonOf(data)`, the one parser of an `INVALID_STATE` error's `data`. The sponsor pages' `invalidStateReason`, the admin's `sponsorshipActionError` and `@smog/admin/client`'s `sponsorshipRefusalOf` all use them; the two site copies are gone.
- **2026-10-03 · A link refused for good is a dead end with its reason, never a Retry (review I-1).** `LinkState` answers an `INVALID_STATE` from `renewal.get` / `reedit.get` (for example `notRenewable` after a force expire) with a level-1 `EmptyState`: "Link no longer usable" (`sponsor.link.unavailable.title`), the reason's copy and the back link. Revoking open tokens on force expire, reject and cancel is server work and is left to the server fix wave. (Done in the phase 6 close-out.)
- **2026-10-03 · A failed availability read says so on step 1 (review I-2), and cards keep their state across reads (review M-6).** `useGridAvailability` returns an `error` with a `retry` over the failed chunks; `StepSelect` shows a level-2 `ErrorState` ("Availability could not be checked", Retry) above the grid while the cards stay visible and disabled. TanStack's `keepPreviousData` cannot help here: `useQueries` matches observers by query hash, so a chunk whose ids changed is a new observer with no previous data. Instead the hook remembers the last answered item per id (a ref written after render) and uses it until the id's new read answers, so "Load more" and a search keystroke no longer flash the shown cards disabled; the server checks availability again at checkout (`GESTURE_UNAVAILABLE`).
- **2026-10-03 · A Turnstile token lives exactly as long as its widget (review M-3).** `useTurnstileToken` (site, `components/sponsor`) holds the token and the widget's reset key: `reset` runs after a failed attempt, on every wizard step change (the widget lives on step 3 only), and on a bfcache `pageshow` with `persisted`, so Pay never sends a token that expired or was already spent. The renewal page uses the same hook.
- **2026-10-03 · Renewal Pay stays busy while the browser leaves (review M-1).** `RenewalView` uses `busy = isPending || redirecting` for `loading` and returns early in `pay`, as the wizard does.
- **2026-10-03 · A failed renewal payment has its own copy (review M-2).** `sponsor.success.failed.renewalDescription`: nothing was charged and the sponsorship runs on until its current end; use the email's link to try again. It carries no date: `paymentStatus` sends the end only for a paid renewal (`renewedUntil`), and adding it for a failed one is server work. (Superseded by the phase 6 close-out: `paymentStatus` now sends `endsAt` and the copy names the date.)
- **2026-10-03 · Bars reserve their height as scroll padding together (review M-4).** `useReservedSpace` moved from the consent banner to `apps/site/src/lib/use-reserved-space.ts` and sums every mounted bar's height into `scroll-padding-bottom`, so the consent banner and the wizard's sticky selection bar can show at once. The selection bar uses it on step 1 (WCAG 2.4.11).
- **2026-10-03 · No global Playwright `actionTimeout` (review M-8).** In the installed Playwright it becomes the context's default timeout, which also caps `request` calls (the seeds, the RPC helpers), `waitForFunction` (`waitForApp`, `waitForHydration`) and `waitForResponse`. It is removed; the catalogue keyboard reorder bounds each of its waits explicitly with `STEP_MS` (the focus, the pressed state, the announcements, the reorder response, the final order). Navigations keep `navigationTimeout` 0 (the test's 60 s).
- **2026-10-03 · The phase 6 guard is a repo-level script test (review M-9).** `scripts/phase6-guard.test.ts` (run by `test:scripts` in the tests lane) replaces `packages/features/sponsorships/src/phase6-pending.test.ts`: it scans the sponsorship server recursively for a `notImplemented(` call, keeps `apps/site/e2e/phase6.ts` and the `PHASE6_PENDING` flag out, and fails on any `skip`/`fixme`/`only`/`fail` call in a sponsor spec (`sponsor*.spec.ts`, `admin-sponsorships*.spec.ts`) other than the review-screenshot switches it lists line for line.
- **2026-10-03 · `EMAIL_NOT_SENT` has its own message, and a repeated sign-up resends the link (jobs review M-2).** The client maps `EMAIL_NOT_SENT` to `auth.errors.emailNotSent` ("We couldn't send the email. Please try again in a moment.", nl/en/fr) instead of the generic error. On the server, `emailAndPassword.onExistingUserSignUp` (Better Auth 1.7.6, called only on the generic-duplicate path) marks an existing **unverified** account in a request state, and the `after` hook of `/sign-up/email` mails it a fresh link with Better Auth's own `sendVerificationEmailFn(ctx, user)` (its token, its URL and the body's `callbackURL`). So "try again" is literally the recovery after a sign-up whose first email never left; a failed resend answers `503 EMAIL_NOT_SENT` like any other send. A verified account gets nothing, the answer is Better Auth's generic one either way (no enumeration), and the path stays behind Turnstile and `RL_AUTH`. The password sent with the repeated sign-up is ignored (the account keeps its first one); the link signs the user in (`autoSignInAfterVerification`), and a forgotten password goes through the reset.
- **2026-10-03 · axe never scans the Mux player's own element.** In a sandbox without Mux, or with the stubbed playlist that has no segments, `<mux-player>` can open its built-in error dialog. That dialog has no accessible name, and whether it is open when axe runs depends on timing, so `admin-sponsorships-a11y.spec.ts` sometimes failed on `aria-dialog-name`. Every axe scan in the e2e now excludes the `mux-player` element and its shadow DOM: `blockingViolations` in `e2e/helpers.ts`, and the two `/dev/ui` specs through `withoutAxeExcluded`. It is a third-party component we do not author. Our own markup around it (the wrapper, its label and the poster) is still scanned. Stubbing the network would not help, because the player's error UI also opens on the stub.

## Phase 6 close-out

- **2026-10-04 · Ending a sponsorship revokes its open links (UI review I-1, server half).** `reject`, `forceExpire` and `cancel` (for each sponsorship it moves to `cancelled`) append `revokeAllTokensStatement` (`@smog/sponsorships/server` `statements.ts`: `used_at = now` on every open token of the sponsorship, re-edit and renewal alike) to the same D1 batch as the guarded transition, so the change, its event, the revocation and the admin's audit entry land together or not at all; a lost race revokes nothing. The emailed link then reads as `invalid` and answers the existing `TOKEN_INVALID`, which `LinkState` already shows as "link no longer usable"; no new error and no migration. Cancelling a **renewal** payment changes no sponsorship and leaves its renewal link open, so the sponsor can try again. The audit guard is unaffected: it counts `audit_log` inserts, and the revocation is an `UPDATE` of `sponsorship_token`.
- **2026-10-04 · A failed renewal names the date it runs until (UI review M-2, server half).** `paymentStatus` answers `endsAt` (the sponsorship's unchanged `ends_at`, epoch ms) for a renewal payment that is `failed`, `canceled` or `expired`, next to `renewedUntil` for a paid one; an initial payment gets neither. `sponsor.success.failed.renewalDescription` (nl/en/fr) now carries `{{date}}`, formatted with `formatDate` in the page locale like `renewedUntil`; the undated copy stays as `renewalDescriptionUndated` for an answer without `endsAt`. This supersedes the "no date" note of the UI fix wave's M-2 entry.

## Foundations: dependencies, the render contract, env, boundaries, migration 0011 (phase 7 task 1)

- **2026-10-04 · [dep] Remotion 4.0.532, pinned exactly and all on one version.** `remotion`, `@remotion/player`, `@remotion/renderer`, `@remotion/bundler`, `@remotion/layout-utils` and `@remotion/fonts` are in the root catalog at `4.0.532` (the registry's latest on 2026-10-04; Remotion requires every package on the same version, so none takes a range). `@smog/render` depends on `remotion`, `react`/`react-dom` (its peers), `@remotion/fonts`, `@remotion/layout-utils` and `@remotion/renderer` (the server runtime, kept by the image's `--production` install); `@remotion/bundler` is a devDependency (the bundle is built with the image). `@remotion/player` is the site's. `@remotion/cli`, `@remotion/studio` and `@remotion/media` are not used (ruling 5). Licence: Remotion is free for non-profits and companies of up to 3 people (https://www.remotion.dev/docs/license); SMOG & Co vzw's status is an owner confirmation item (ruling 14), and `REMOTION_LICENSE_KEY` is an optional secret for the case it is needed.
- **2026-10-04 · [dep] `mediabunny` 1.61.1, `@fontsource/inter` 5.3.0, `@cloudflare/containers` 0.3.7.** `mediabunny` reads the source's duration and size in Bun and the browser (`./metadata`, task 3); Inter 600 is the overlay font (OFL-1.1, ruling 6); `@cloudflare/containers` is the `SmogRenderer` base class, in the site only (task 2). All newest on the registry on 2026-10-04.
- **2026-10-04 · [dep] Cloudflare tooling bumped: `wrangler` 4.143.0 → 4.147.0, `@cloudflare/vitest-plugin` 1.3.1 → 1.3.6, `@cloudflare/vite-plugin` 1.62.0 → 1.62.5.** The Vite plugin moves from a pin in `apps/site` into the catalog with the other two. The whole gate passes on them.
- **2026-10-04 · Dependencies declared ahead of their import.** Task 1 owns the root manifest for the phase, so the catalog holds every phase 7 dependency now, and knip fails on a catalog entry nobody references. Each is therefore declared in the package that will import it (`@smog/render`: `@remotion/fonts`, `@remotion/layout-utils`, `mediabunny`, `@remotion/renderer`; `@smog/site`: `@remotion/player`, `@cloudflare/containers`), with a named `ignoreDependencies` entry in `knip.json` for each. The task that first imports one (3, 4, 2, 8) deletes its line; knip's "unused ignore" hint points at any that are left. Alternative: `ignoreIssues` for `catalog` on `package.json` (rejected: it would hide every stale catalog entry for good).
- **2026-10-04 · The render server's HTTP contract is in `./contract`.** `renderRequestSchema` is `{ v: 1, renderJobId: uuid, input: renderInputSchema, sourceUrl, logoDataUrl, uploadUrl }`; both URLs must parse with an `http`/`https` scheme (the server narrows to `https:` and `*.mux.com` unless `RENDER_ALLOW_HTTP`, ruling 7). `logoDataUrl` is `null` or a `data:image/(png|jpeg|webp);base64,…` URL whose payload is whole base64 quads and decodes to at most `RENDER_LOGO_MAX_BYTES` (2 MiB, the checkout's `LOGO_MAX_BYTES`, restated because `@smog/render` may not import a feature). `renderResultSchema` is `{ ok: true, frames, width, height, bytes, ms }` (even sizes, H.264) or `{ ok: false, code, message }`, with `message` at most `RENDER_ERROR_MESSAGE_MAX` (2000) characters, so a stack trace cannot bloat a step output. `RENDER_ERROR_STATUS` gives each code its HTTP status: `invalidInput`, `sourceUnreadable` and `logoUnreadable` 422 (final), `busy` 503, `renderFailed` 500 and `uploadFailed` 502 (retried); `isRetryableRenderError` reads it, so status and retryability cannot drift apart. `RendererPort` (`render(request) → RenderResult`, a fault throws) is a type in `./contract`, so the sponsorships Workflow logic takes it without `./testing`. `SPONSORED_VIDEO_ID = "SponsoredVideo"`, `RENDER_FPS = 30`. The layout comment now says line 1's **top** is at `y` (ruling 5).
- **2026-10-04 · The fake renderer validates like the server.** `@smog/render/testing` `createFakeRenderer({ result | error | delayMs })` records every request and validates it first: one that `renderRequestSchema` refuses gets `invalidInput` at once, before `delayMs` and `error`, as the real server answers 422 before rendering (so a Workflow test cannot pass with a request the Container would refuse). Otherwise it answers `result` (a value or a function of the request; a 5 s 1080 × 1920 success by default) or throws `error` after `delayMs`.
- **2026-10-04 · The render env (ruling 11).** `RENDER_LOCAL_URL` is an optional `http`/`https` var, unset means `RENDER_LOCAL_DEFAULT_URL` (`http://127.0.0.1:3002`), and `workerEnvSchema` refuses any value of it outside dev; a schema default would have made "refused outside dev" impossible to express. `RENDER_MODE=local` is refused outside dev too. `REMOTION_LICENSE_KEY` is an optional secret. `parseWorkerBindings` checks `RENDER_WORKFLOW` (`create`, `get`) and `RENDERER` (`idFromName`, `get`) only when present and answers them as optional (`OPTIONAL_WORKER_BINDINGS`), so a build without them (every env today) starts as before. `requiredWorkerConfig(env, renderMode)` adds `MUX_TOKEN_ID`, `MUX_TOKEN_SECRET` and `MUX_WEBHOOK_SECRET` for `container`, without duplicates; `REQUIRED_WORKER_CONFIG` is that function at each env's mode in `wrangler.jsonc` (`fake`, `fake`, `container`), so its values are unchanged.
- **2026-10-04 · `@smog/config/env/render` (`renderServerEnvSchema`).** `PORT` (default 8080, 1..65535), `RENDER_TMP_DIR` (`/tmp/smog-render`) and `RENDER_BUNDLE_DIR` (`/app/bundle`) as absolute paths, `REMOTION_LICENSE_KEY?`, `RENDER_BROWSER_EXECUTABLE?`, `RENDER_ENVIRONMENT` (the Worker passes it; unset is `production`, the strictest reading) and `RENDER_ALLOW_HTTP` (`1`/`true`/`0`/`false`/empty; any other value is a typo and refused), which is refused unless `RENDER_ENVIRONMENT=dev`. The package's `serve` script sets `PORT=3002 RENDER_ENVIRONMENT=dev RENDER_ALLOW_HTTP=1`.
- **2026-10-04 · `@smog/render/testing` joins the feature packages' boundaries (ruling 1)**, as `@smog/analytics/testing` did; a feature still reaches only `./contract` and `./testing` of `@smog/render`, never `./composition`, `./metadata` or `./remotion` (test).
- **2026-10-04 · Migration 0011 `render_job_status_idx`.** `render_job_status_updated_idx` on `(status, updated_at)` for the watchdog (ruling 12); `CREATE INDEX` only. A test applies 0000–0010 and the dev seed, adds a sponsorship and a job, applies 0011, and checks the job is unchanged and that `WHERE status = ? AND updated_at < ? ORDER BY updated_at LIMIT 50` is a `SEARCH … USING INDEX render_job_status_updated_idx (status=? AND updated_at<?)` with no temp B-tree, so the watchdog reads one status at a time. The `user.welcomed_at` carry now takes 0012 (PROGRESS).
- **2026-10-04 · The bundling smoke (Minor 15).** `src/compositions` holds a placeholder `SponsoredVideo` (the contract layout, the tokens' green, and the two Inter 600 `.woff2` imports in an `@font-face`), registered by `src/remotion` with `calculateMetadata` handing back the props' size and duration. `test/bundle.test.ts` runs `@remotion/bundler` `bundle()` on that entry and Vite's `build()` on `./composition`, both into temp directories, and checks that each emits the two font files and references them by URL (and, for Remotion, that the contract and the token colour came through the workspace `exports`). No Chrome. Vite is checked in app mode, as the site builds: library mode inlines every asset as base64, which says nothing about the site. Task 3 replaces the placeholder and its `@font-face` with `@remotion/fonts` `loadFont`. `SponsoredVideoProps` is an `interface` that extends `Record<string, unknown>`, because `<Composition>` takes props of that shape (Biome's `useConsistentTypeDefinitions` keeps it an interface).
