## Toolchain: Biome, Bun and the newest-stable sweep (phase 9 task 10)

Ruling 13. Each group below is its own commit, each one gated by `check:ci`, `boundaries`, `knip`, `check-types`, `build` (the bundle budget guard), the staging `deploy:dry` hash and the whole `bun run test`, so a failing group can be reverted alone. The built staging `wrangler.json` hash is unchanged by every group (`5c706b1c16adc89e253bdeaa5d73c1c36b1bbf1438aece8f0ba9799e66fc30bb`).

- **2026-10-20 · [dep] Biome stays on 2.5.14 (2.5.15 tried again).** On the current tree 2.5.15 reports **25** `suspicious/noUnnecessaryConditions` errors and nothing else: the same 24 sites as in phase 8 task 1 (`query-persist.ts:194` is reported twice), so the false positives are unchanged. Sites: TanStack Query `isPlaceholderData`/`isPending`/`data` in the admin tables, `use-gesture-search.ts`, the mobile home and search screens, `query-persist.ts`, `auth/src/server.ts`, `gestures/hooks.test.tsx`. That is far more than "a handful", and a blanket turn-off would lose the rule's true positives, so the bump is not taken (the brief's stop rule). Still waiting on a Biome release on which `biome lint --only=suspicious/noUnnecessaryConditions .` is clean on these sites; the next dependency pass checks it. No upstream issue number is recorded: this machine cannot reach the Biome tracker.
- **2026-10-20 · [dep] `ultracite` 7.12.2 → 8.0.1 (a major preset), taken.** Its preset enables `correctness/noUndeclaredDependencies`; the only five hits are in `scripts/__fixtures__/boundaries/**`, which are deliberately broken packages (they import what their manifests do not declare, for the boundaries test). `biome.json` turns that one rule off for `scripts/__fixtures__/**` by name (an override; no other rule changed). `bunx biome ci .` is otherwise clean on Biome 2.5.14 (the preset allows `^2.5.0`) and formats nothing.
- **2026-10-20 · Bun stays on 1.3.11; the upgrade is the owner's, with a checklist.** What was proven here: `bun` 1.4.3 is the newest stable (npm `bun@1.4.3`; the GitHub release page still answers 403 through the proxy, so the binary came from the npm package). In a clean `git archive` of this branch, 1.4.3 `bun install --frozen-lockfile` exits 0 and leaves `bun.lock` byte-identical, a plain `bun install` changes nothing, and `bun run test` exits 0 (all packages, 21 min on this machine). What cannot be proven here: the render image (no Docker daemon, so no build of `packages/render/container/Dockerfile`, no `release:check:render`, and the Chrome-in-container behaviour under Bun 1.4), and CI's `oven-sh/setup-bun` resolving 1.4.3 from `packageManager`. `packageManager` is therefore **not changed**. Checklist for the owner (also in Task 11's list):
  1. Digests looked up on 2026-10-20 (registry API, `Docker-Content-Digest`; the 1.3.11 values match the Dockerfile today, which proves the lookup): `oven/bun:1.4.3` = `sha256:ec06c3b6cea04192ae6770c434f668ca41d343ad19fa6472216c7b48be39c598`, `oven/bun:1.4.3-slim` = `sha256:14a223fa35747ec728bce300ef85888bc5f7958164d15bbba6a54b22b52a3288`. Re-fetch before using them (`docker buildx imagetools inspect oven/bun:1.4.3 --format '{{json .Manifest.Digest}}'`, same for `1.4.3-slim`; or `docker pull` and read `RepoDigests`).
  2. `packages/render/container/Dockerfile` has **five** `FROM` lines, not three: `prune` (line 18), `build` (26), `deps` (40) and `sources` (50) use `oven/bun:1.3.11@sha256:0733e50325078969732ebe3b15ce4c4be5082f18c4ac1a0f0ca4839c2e4e42a7`, and `runtime` (60) uses `oven/bun:1.3.11-slim@sha256:478281fdd196871c7e51ba6a820b7803a8ae97042ec86cdbc2e1c6b6626442d9`. Replace the tag and digest on all five.
  3. Set `"packageManager": "bun@1.4.3"` in the root `package.json` (CI's `setup-bun` reads it), `bun install` and commit the lockfile if it changes (it did not here), and `bun add -d @types/bun@1.4.3` is already the installed type version.
  4. Run `bun run release:check:render` on a machine with Docker (or let CI's render lane run), then `bun run release:check`, and watch the first CI e2e and render lanes on the branch before merging to `develop`.
  5. Revert path: the two files above, nothing else.
- **2026-10-20 · The sweep: what was bumped** (eight groups, one commit each). Dev tooling: `knip` 6.39.0 → 6.42.1, `turbo` 2.11.7 → 2.11.9, `@types/bun` 1.4.2 → 1.4.3, `ultracite` (above), `@happy-dom/global-registrator` 20.14.5 → 21.0.3 (a major, but its only use is the `bun test` preload; every happy-dom suite passes unchanged). Cloudflare and test runner: `wrangler` 4.147.0 → 4.151.3, `@cloudflare/vite-plugin` 1.62.5 → 1.64.0, `@cloudflare/vitest-plugin` 1.3.6 → 1.4.6, `vite` 8.3.1 → 8.3.4. React side: `@tanstack/react-query`, `query-core` and the two mobile persisters 5.104.1 → 5.104.5 (the two catalog lines moved together, as the phase 8 entry requires), `@tanstack/react-router` 1.170.41 → 1.170.45, `@tanstack/react-start` 1.168.60 → 1.169.3, `lucide-react` and `lucide-react-native` 1.52.0 → 1.60.0, `radix-ui` 1.6.7 → 1.8.1, `@mux/mux-player-react` 3.13.4 → 3.14.0, `react-email` 6.11.0 → 6.11.3, `@testing-library/user-event` 14.6.8, `@vitejs/plugin-react` 6.1.2, `react-native-reorderable-list` 0.18.2. Backend: `better-auth` and its three packages 1.7.7 → 1.7.12, oRPC (six packages) 1.15.4 → 1.15.7, `zod` 4.6.5 → 4.6.7, `drizzle-orm` 0.45.3 → 0.45.4, `i18next` 26.5.3, `react-i18next` 17.1.0. Render: `remotion` and the five `@remotion/*` packages 4.0.532 → 4.0.537 and `mediabunny` 1.61.1 → 1.61.6; `bun run test:render` (real Chrome, headless shell) passes 3/3 on them, the render lane itself (Docker) is CI only. Native test libraries: `@testing-library/react-native` 14.0.1 → 14.1.0, `test-renderer` 1.2.0 → 1.3.1 (mobile and ui-native suites unchanged). `babel-preset-expo` moved 57.0.13 → 57.0.16 inside its existing `~57.0.13` range when the lockfile was refreshed.

  - **`vitest` 5.0.5** (4.1.11 stays): `check-types`, `build` and every package except the site pass, but `@smog/site`'s Workers-pool suite dies with `WebSocket protocol error; statusCode = 1009; Message is too large` and only 3 of 44 files complete. The `@cloudflare/vitest-plugin` 1.4.x peer range allows 5, so the break is in the pool's transport with this suite's payloads. Retry when the plugin or Vitest ships a fix; the commit is not kept.

  - **Biome 2.5.15**: above.
- **2026-10-20 · The sweep: held on purpose** (each keeps its earlier entry): React and React DOM 19.2.3 → 19.3.1 with `@types/react` 19.3.0, `@types/react-dom` 19.3.0 and `react-test-renderer` 19.3.1 (Expo SDK 57 pins 19.2.3); the whole Expo SDK 58 line (`expo` 58.0.16 and every `expo-*`, `babel-preset-expo` 58.0.18, `jest-expo` 58.0.11), React Native 0.86.3 → 0.88.0 and `@react-native/jest-preset`, Reanimated 4.7.1, Gesture Handler 3.4.0, Screens 4.30.0, Safe Area 5.10.1, SVG 15.16.0, WebView 14.0.1, Worklets 0.13.0, Async Storage 3.1.1 (all are SDK pins: `bundledNativeModules.json` of SDK 57 lists SVG 15.15.4 and Async Storage 2.2.0, and the others are the same kind), Jest 29.7.0 → 30.5.2 with `babel-jest` and `@jest/globals` (`jest-expo` 57 needs Jest 29), Tailwind 3.4.19 → 4.3.3 in the mobile packages (NativeWind 4 needs Tailwind 3; the web theme already runs Tailwind 4), `expo-doctor` 1.20.4 → 1.21.5 (its checks run online in CI only, cannot be tried here, and a newer doctor may check against SDK 58). Expo SDK 57 patch pins: `bunx expo install --check` could not run here (api.expo.dev answers 403 through the proxy); the Expo-managed packages were **not** changed, `bundledNativeModules.json` was compared by hand for the ones that appear there, and CI's expo-doctor stays the authority.
- **2026-10-20 · Two traps of a dependency sweep in this repository (for the next one).** (1) `bun install` after a catalog bump can leave a stale nested copy of a package that is also hoisted (`drizzle-orm` under `@smog/auth` and `@smog/db`, `@orpc/*` and `zod` under `@smog/api`, a nested `vitest/vite`), which splits TypeScript's types (a Drizzle or Vite type is no longer the same type) while the versions match; deleting those `"@smog/<pkg>/<dep>"` lines from `bun.lock` and `node_modules`, or removing `node_modules` and running `bun install --frozen-lockfile`, gives one copy. The committed lockfile has them removed; a frozen install is clean. (2) Every package keeps its own `node_modules/.cache/tsc` (`tsBuildInfoFile` in `@smog/config/tsconfig/library.json`), and a stale one repeats an old type error after the dependency was fixed: after a dependency change delete all of them (`find . -path '*/node_modules/.cache/tsc' -prune -exec rm -rf {} +`), not only the root one.

## Docs final pass, the owner list and the phase close (phase 9 task 11)

**The phase 9 index.** One section per task, in this file, each under its own heading:

| Task | What | Section |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Playwright e2e as an always-on CI job, seven shards in the end, `bun run test:e2e` | "Playwright e2e as a CI job (phase 9 task 1)" |
| 2 | Importer `apply --reset-only`, a preflight that accounts for `--reset`, `we-moved --resend`, turbo before the site deploy | "Tooling carries: importer reset-only, `we-moved --resend`, the turbo deploy path (phase 9 task 2)" |
| 3 | Mobile `APP_VARIANT` builds, the accessibility pass, the sign-out confirmation | "Mobile: app variants, the accessibility pass and the sign-out confirmation (phase 9 task 3)" |
| 4 | Dead letters in D1 (migration 0013), both DLQ consumers, admin replay and discard, the daily digest | "Dead letters, replay and alerting (phase 9 task 4)" |
| 5 | Web accessibility: axe on every public state, keyboard, reflow and motion runs; the a11y shard split | "Web accessibility: axe on every public state, keyboard, reflow and motion runs (phase 9 task 5)" and "a11y shard on CI (phase 9 task 5 follow-up)" |
| 6 | Performance: bundle budgets in the deploy guard, lazy splits, the cache policy, query plans, migration 0014 | "Performance: budgets, lazy splits, cache policy and query plans (phase 9 task 6)" |
| 7 | Re-edit and renewal tokens in the URL fragment | "Re-edit and renewal tokens in the URL fragment (phase 9 task 7)" |
| 8 | Security review: authorization table, body cap, header set, log sampling, route guard | "Security review (phase 9 task 8)" |
| 9 | The parity audit: every inventory row closed, the manifest and `/assets/logo.svg` | "Parity audit (phase 9 task 9)" |
| 10 | Toolchain: Biome and Bun kept, the newest-stable sweep, the owner's Bun checklist | "Toolchain: Biome, Bun and the newest-stable sweep (phase 9 task 10)" |
| 11 | This section: the documents' accuracy check, the owner list's final form, the R2 design | (below) |

- **2026-10-20 · The documents are checked by a test (`scripts/docs-check.test.ts`, in `bun run test`).** It reads AGENTS.md, README.md and every `docs/*.md` except DECISIONS (which is history: a phase 9 section says "the next free number is 0013" on purpose) and fails on: a `bun run <script>` or `bun -F @smog/<pkg> <script>` that no `package.json` defines; a relative link or anchor that does not resolve; a repository path in backticks (or inside a fenced command) under `apps/`, `packages/`, `scripts/`, `docs/`, `patches/` or `.github/`, or a root file name, that does not exist; a quoted `DECISIONS "title"` that matches no heading or bold entry title; a "next free migration number" that is not the real one (the highest `NNNN_*.sql` plus one); and AGENTS.md's CI paragraph against `ci.yml` (every `check:` lane and `shard:` name present, "four lanes", "five jobs", the shard count in words, and the `--project a|b|…` usage list equal to the matrix). **Ignored paths are recognised with `git check-ignore`**, not listed (`.dev.vars`, `.wrangler/`, `dist/`, `worker-configuration.d.ts`), so the allow-list is short and every entry carries its reason: the analysis folder and the plans (`docs/superpowers/…` is in the tree, but a plan is named by phase before it exists), turbo's own `docs/README.md`, which the block turbo writes into AGENTS.md names, and `apps/site/e2e/phase6.ts`, a file deleted in phase 6 that the history log names. Each checker is a pure function over text, and the file proves that every one of them fails on a deliberately broken document (a broken link, a broken anchor, a missing path in prose and in a fence, an unknown script, an unknown DECISIONS title, a wrong migration number, a CI name `ci.yml` does not have). **What it cannot check, and what was read by hand instead** (the task 11 report lists the pass): counts and figures in prose (test counts, sizes, times), the claims about what a screen does, dashboard names, and a path written without its top-level directory (`src/worker/…`). Alternative rejected: a long hand-kept allow-list (it rots; `git check-ignore` is the source of truth the repository already has) and a markdown AST (the checks are line-based on purpose, so a failure names the line).
- **2026-10-20 · The owner list is one ordered list with its summary on top.** `docs/PROGRESS.md` keeps every earlier step of "Owner actions in order" in its order and adds the phase 9 items where their prerequisites are met: the `e2e` required check with the first real CI run (a new first step, because every later push depends on it), the Cloudflare notifications with the Access bypass, the real-device passes and the link-scanner check with the first production deploy, the dead-letter retention row in the legal sign-off, the variants' Apple, Google and EAS setup with the stores, the Bun upgrade checklist after launch (post-launch carries). The top of the section is a one-screen "what is still blocked on you" summary that names each blocker once with its step number; the steps carry the detail and their place in `docs/deployment.md` or the runbook. Alternative rejected: a second, separate checklist (two lists drift).
- **2026-10-20 · R2 jurisdiction: the design for a later task (ruling 8 keeps it unsupported).** If the owner wants the production bucket in an EU jurisdiction (`--jurisdiction eu`), the change is small and local, and none of it is built: (1) a new optional var `R2_JURISDICTION` (empty or `eu`; any other value refused) in `workerEnvSchema`, not in `BASE_REQUIRED_CONFIG` (it is optional, like a location hint); (2) one helper in `@smog/config`, `r2Endpoint(accountId, jurisdiction)`, returning `https://<account>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com` or `https://<account>.eu.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`, used by `packages/features/sponsorships/src/server/logo.ts` (the presigned PUT host; the signing region stays `auto`) and by `buildCsp`'s `connect-src` in `apps/site/src/worker/headers.ts`, so the two cannot differ (one test compares them); (3) `--jurisdiction eu` (`-J eu`) on every bucket command of `scripts/ensure-cloudflare-resources.ts` (`list`, `create`, `cors list`, `cors set`), because today a jurisdiction bucket is invisible to `list` and the script would create a second default bucket of the same name; (4) `scripts/check-deploy-config.ts` refuses a value outside the list; (5) the `docs/deployment.md` §5 sentence becomes "set `R2_JURISDICTION` before the first deploy"; (6) tests: the signed URL's host per jurisdiction, the CSP equality, the ensure script's command lines (the wrangler fake) and the deploy config check. It touches `headers.ts` (a guarded file: `test/security-headers.test.ts` pins the header set per env) and the sponsorships server, which is why it was not done inside the hardening phase for a bucket that holds no personal data (company logos of at most 2 MB; the personal data is in D1 and Mux). **It must be decided before production's bucket exists, because a bucket's location and jurisdiction cannot change afterwards.** An existing default bucket would stay and be replaced by creating a jurisdiction bucket, copying the `logos/` objects (a few megabytes) and pointing the var at it.
- **2026-10-20 · HSTS has no `preload`.** `Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains` (task 8) is set on every Worker response and on the static files. Preload needs an apex domain the owner controls and a submission to the preload list, which is a one-way door for every subdomain; on workers.dev there is nothing to submit, and on path D the site is a subdomain of `smog.vlaanderen`, so the decision is the owner's, with the zone's other subdomains in mind. Recorded as an owner decision (post-launch), not an omission.
- **2026-10-20 · Entries superseded or amended by phase 9 are marked where they stand.** Each carries a one-line "Amended (phase 9 task 11)" note that says what changed and where: the token pages' log note (fragment tokens), the phase 6 queue and DLQ entry (the DLQs now have consumers), the importer's "no reset-only mode", "Mobile builds share one identity" (superseded by `APP_VARIANT`) and "`we-moved` has no replay tool yet" (`--resend` and the dead-letter replay). The original text is kept, because it says why the earlier choice was made.
- **2026-10-20 · The dead-letter e2e seed gets a unique idempotency key (found by the final three-in-a-row run).** `deadLetterBody` in `apps/site/src/server/e2e-seed.ts` seeded the replayable email with the fixed key `e2e-dead-letter:<id>`. The email consumer keeps an `email:sent:<key>` marker in KV for 7 days, and a local run resets D1 but not KV, so the second run of the `admin` project on one machine replayed the message into a silent repeat: no email reached the dev mailbox and `admin-dead-letters.spec.ts:93` failed twice (CI is unaffected: every shard starts on a fresh runner). The key now ends in a random UUID per seeding (the unit test matches the pattern); nothing else in the spec changed. A test of idempotency itself stays in the consumer's own tests.
- **2026-10-20 · The phase is marked "review pending", not done.** `docs/PROGRESS.md` ticks phase 9 only once the phase review's fix wave is merged; the log line for this task says what is pending. What the owner still has to do is unchanged by it.

## Phase 9 review fix wave

- **2026-10-20 · An admin's user deletion erases the dead letters too (review 1).** The by-address erase (`eraseDeadLettersOf`, `@smog/db`) is shared: the self-service path (`account.delete`) and `admin.users.delete` both call it after Better Auth removed the user. The admin handler reads the email before `removeUser` (the row is gone afterwards). A failure is logged (`[admin] Failed to erase the dead letters of the deleted user:<id>`) and not rethrown, as on the self-service path: the account is already deleted, the audit entry must still be written, and the 14-day retention removes the row anyway. Tested in `admin/test/users.test.ts` (by address, case-insensitive; other recipients and body-less rows stay).
- **2026-10-20 · The e2e dev server binds loopback outside CI (review 2).** `playwright.config.ts` starts `vite dev --host 0.0.0.0` only when `CI` is set (the runners resolve `localhost` to `::1` first) and `--host 127.0.0.1` otherwise, so the unauthenticated dev seed endpoint is not reachable from a developer's network while `test:e2e` runs.
- **2026-10-20 · The digest never drops a loss counted after its read (review 3).** KV has no compare-and-delete. After the marker is written, each lost counter is read again: unchanged it is deleted, grown it is rewritten with the difference (the reported part comes off, a later loss stays for the next report). A loss landing between that second read and the write is the one window left (milliseconds, and the `[dlq] lost` log line exists either way); a Durable Object counter would close it and is not worth a new binding. Tested with a queue whose `send` raises the counter mid-run.
- **2026-10-20 · The sign-in icon is back at normal widths (review 4).** The 320 px reflow change had hidden the `LogIn` icon everywhere but below 360 px. It shows again, and the label gives way to it below 360 px (`sr-only`, still the link's accessible name). One band stays text-only, 360 to 420 px: with the icon the header needs 405 px at 390 under WCAG 1.4.12's text-spacing override and overflowed (six `a11y-reflow` failures), so a spacing-proof 390 px phone wins over the icon there. Three reflow specs pin the widths.
- **2026-10-20 · The emailed `?token=` links are redirected on the first request (review 5).** `/sponsor/edit?token=` and `/sponsor/renew?token=` (every reminder sent before phase 9) get the same no-store 302 to `#token=` as the legacy `/sponsors/*` paths, before the app renders, so the token is not in a cached response or a `Referer` of the first load. The entries' `to` equals their `from`, so they redirect only with a non-empty `token` (without one the page is current: no loop, no 301). Old links keep working (the redirect lands on the fragment form; the client-side move stays as the fallback). Amends the phase 9 task 7 entry. Workers Logs still record the one redirected request's URL, as for the legacy paths.

## Brand and design system foundations (phase 10a task 1)

- **2026-10-21 · Ruling 1, the colour vocabulary (aliases).** `tokens.ts` now holds shadcn's names (`card`, `popover`, `secondary`, `muted`, `accent`, `destructive`, `input`, `ring`, the `sidebar-*` and `chart-1` to `chart-5` sets, `*-foreground`) next to the phase 1 to 9 names. `ALIASES` lists the old names that mean a new one (`surface` = `card`, `surfaceRaised` = `popover`, `foregroundMuted` = `mutedForeground`, `danger` = `destructive`, `focusRing` = `ring`); a test holds their values equal in both themes, so a screen on the old name and a registry component on the new one cannot disagree. `surfaceSunken`, `borderSubtle`, `primaryStrong`, `primarySubtle` and the `*Strong`/`*Subtle` status roles stay as roles of their own. Task 9 removes the aliases.
- **2026-10-21 · `accent` means shadcn's `accent` now; the orange is `highlight`.** In shadcn `accent` is the quiet hover and selected-row fill (menus, tabs, the command palette), so registry code written for it must not turn orange. `accent` is a green tint (`#E4F1EA`, dark `#17352A`) with `accentForeground` the text colour; the brand orange is `highlight` with `highlightForeground`. Only the `Badge` `accent` variant used the old meaning; it keeps its name and now draws `bg-highlight text-highlight-foreground` (web and native). Alternative rejected: keeping `accent` orange, because every registry menu item would highlight in orange.
- **2026-10-21 · The colour system.** Primary stays the brand green `#00805F` (white on it measures 4.94:1, AA body text); `primaryStrong` is the deeper green `#006B50` for text on warm neutrals and for pressed states; the orange is `#EE971C`, never text on a light surface. Neutrals are warm with a faint green tint (`background` `#FAF9F4`, `muted` `#F2F0E8`, `border` `#DCD8CB`); dark mode is a deep green-black (`background` `#0C110E`, `card` `#131914`, `popover` `#1A211C`). **Green surfaces:** `bg-primary text-primary-foreground` (the filled green: hero, auth, buttons; secondary text on it is `primary-foreground-muted`, focus ring `primary-ring`) and `bg-sidebar text-sidebar-foreground` (the deeper green of the header and the admin sidebar; hover `sidebar-accent`, the active mark `sidebar-primary` in orange, focus ring `sidebar-ring` in white). In dark mode `primary` stays the bright green with near-black text (white would measure 2.4:1) and `sidebar` is the deep green `#0F2A21` with light text, so both surfaces keep their meaning. `input` is the form-control border (3:1 on every neutral), because the decorative `border` is not a UI boundary. The light `ring` is a darker orange `#B36B0B` (the brand orange is 2.19:1 on white; 3:1 needed); it was `#C3780F`, and lowering it keeps 3:1 on the new tints. **Contrast:** `contrast.ts` gained every pair above, among them the text-on-green pairs and green on every warm neutral (`primary` on the neutrals and `accent` at 3:1, `primaryStrong` at 4.5:1); every role is in a pair or in `NON_TEXT_ROLES` (`border`, `borderSubtle`, `sidebarBorder`). `danger` and `dangerStrong` are held on the page backgrounds only, not on the `accent` tint (dark `danger` measures below 4.5:1 on it).
- **2026-10-21 · Ruling 2, Tailwind's default scales return on the web.** The generated `theme.css` no longer resets spacing, radius, type, shadow, easing, breakpoints, containers or font weights. The numeric spacing keys are Tailwind's own (`--spacing: 0.25rem`), so only `--spacing-touch` (44 px) is declared; radius `sm`/`md`/`lg`/`xl`/`full`, the named type steps (`text-body`, `text-title-1`, ...), `shadow-1` to `shadow-3`, `ease-standard`, the durations, `container-content`/`container-reading` and the breakpoints (equal to Tailwind's) are declared on top and override a default of the same name, so existing screens look the same. **The colour namespace stays closed** (`--color-*: initial`), with `white` added: the palette is the semantic roles, never `red-500` (the "tokens-only styling" rule), and registry code only uses the roles. Tested against the real compiler (`tailwind.test.ts`: `size-9`, `text-sm`, `shadow-sm`, `max-w-3xl`, `font-bold` build; `bg-red-500` does not). **Side effect, measured:** 51 classes in the site and the kit that were silent no-ops before now build (admin skeleton heights `h-24`/`h-96`, menu `max-h-80`, `gap-1.5`, `size-3.5`, `max-w-3xl`, ...); they were written for that effect, so the admin screens gain the intended heights and gaps (the admin screenshots change for it). `cn` needed no change (tailwind-merge's defaults and the token extension merge in one group; `cn.test.ts` pins the pairs). The native preset keeps replacing Tailwind 3's scales (task 7 decides the mobile side of ruling 2); it gains the new colour roles automatically, and `kebab` now writes `chart1` as `chart-1`.
- **2026-10-21 · Ruling 7, Figtree.** The variable font (OFL 1.1, `@fontsource-variable/figtree` 5.3.0: `figtree-latin-wght-normal.woff2` 20 kB and `figtree-latin-ext-wght-normal.woff2` 10 kB, weights 300 to 900, `unicode-range` subsets that cover nl, fr and en) is committed under `apps/site/src/fonts/` with its licence, and referenced from `apps/site/src/styles.css`. **Deviation from the brief (`apps/site/public/fonts/`):** Vite hashes the files into `/assets/*.woff2`, which `public/_headers` and `cache.ts` already serve as immutable, so no cache-policy line and no hand-made hashed name is needed; the root route preloads the Latin file through `?url` (`as="font"`, `crossorigin`). `font-display: swap`; **`Figtree Fallback`** is a local Arial with `size-adjust: 100.3%`, `ascent-override: 94.7%`, `descent-override: 24.9%`, `line-gap-override: 0%`, computed from Figtree's metrics (units 1000, hhea 950/-250/0, frequency-weighted average advance 0.4484 em against Arial's 0.4470), so the swap moves nothing. `--font-sans` is `Figtree, "Figtree Fallback", <system stack>`; email keeps the system stack (`fontFamily.email`: clients load no web fonts). The CSP is unchanged (`font-src 'self' data:`). The render container's overlay font (Inter 600) is a render contract and is untouched. **Updating the font:** pack the new `@fontsource-variable/figtree`, replace the two files (same names), re-measure the fallback metrics, run the CLS spec.
- **2026-10-21 · shadcn init: the registry is unreachable from the sandbox; the pin is the GitHub source.** `packages/ui-web/components.json` is committed (style **`radix-maia`**: rounded, generous spacing, which the spec asks for; `rsc: false`, `baseColor: neutral`, `cssVariables: true`, `iconLibrary: lucide`; Tailwind CSS `src/styles.css`; aliases `components`/`ui` `@/components`, `utils` `@/lib/cn` (our `cn`), `lib` `@/lib`, `hooks` `@/hooks`), and `packages/ui-web/tsconfig.json` maps `@/*` to `./src/*`. `bunx shadcn@4.22.1 add button --dry-run` reads the config and then fails on `https://ui.shadcn.com/r/styles/radix-maia/button.json`: the sandbox's proxy answers 403 for `ui.shadcn.com` (the CONNECT is rejected), so the CLI cannot generate here. **What works is a shallow clone of `github.com/shadcn-ui/ui`** (the git proxy serves public repositories): the pin is commit **`ba1503c64472b82256d9de95e2e62c5901a38e8b`** (2026-10-19), the sources are `apps/v4/registry/bases/radix/{ui,hooks,blocks}/` and the style `apps/v4/registry/styles/style-maia.css`. In this registry generation a component is written with `cn-*` placeholder classes (`cn-button`, `cn-button-variant-default`, ...) and `style-maia.css` maps each to its `@apply` list; the CLI replaces them at install, so **a hand port from the pin has to resolve every `cn-*` class from `style-maia.css` and rewrite `from "cn"` to `../lib/cn`** (ruling 3: edit once, keep the kit contracts). The pin is recorded here, not vendored, because the 388 kB of sources are not importable until a component uses them (knip, Biome and `tsc` would each need an exception) and the clone is a one-line command. Task 2 (and 5, 6 for blocks) start from that clone; when the registry becomes reachable, re-running the CLI is a conscious update recorded in this file. After any CLI run, discard its edits to `src/styles.css` (it writes `:root` colour variables that the generated theme already owns) and the `@/` imports it writes must become relative (the site's Vite alias `@` is the site's own `src`).
- **2026-10-21 · Brand outputs.** `bun -F @smog/brand generate` writes four new lockups: `brand/logo-reversed.svg` and `logo-stacked-reversed.svg` (white artwork on a green panel with 0.6 of the artwork height as clear space, for banners and the PDF header) and `logo-mono.svg` and `logo-stacked-mono.svg` (one flat ink colour, `foreground` `#17211A`, on transparent), all under `apps/site/public/brand/` (cached one day by the `/brand/*` rule). The drift test compares them byte for byte. The brand green did not change, so the favicon, icons, OG image, email logo and mobile icons regenerate to identical pixels (the existing drift tests prove it); the store icon is the artwork itself and stays. The email theme reads the same tokens (its roles are unchanged names), so it follows the new neutrals and tints and keeps the green header.

## Mobile foundations and primitives (phase 10a task 7)

- **2026-10-21 · Ruling 2, mobile side: Tailwind 3's default scales return, the token scales extend them.** The native preset (`generate-native.ts`) moved spacing, radius, type size, line height and screens from `theme` to `theme.extend`; only the colours stay a replacement (the semantic roles, `transparent`, `current` and `white`; no raw palette). Tailwind's defaults are rem, and NativeWind's rem is 14 px, so `apps/mobile/metro.config.js` sets `inlineRem: 16` (the web's root size); the token scales are px and unaffected. Registry code (`size-9`, `gap-1.5`, `rounded-2xl`) now compiles unchanged. `classes.test.ts` keeps its job (every utility the kit and the app write must build) with a new off-vocabulary example (`bg-red-500`, `font-light`), and a test that the defaults build.
- **2026-10-21 · Figtree on the apps: four embedded TTF files, one per weight, and weight utilities that pick the file.** `Figtree-Regular|Medium|SemiBold|Bold.ttf` (OFL 1.1, from `@expo-google-fonts/figtree` 0.4.1, renamed to the fonts' PostScript names, which are also the family names on iOS and, as file names, on Android) are in `apps/mobile/assets/fonts` and embedded through the `expo-font` config plugin (no runtime load, so no flash of the system font and no splash gate). A custom font ignores `fontWeight` on Android, so the preset turns off Tailwind's `fontWeight` core plugin and its generated plugin defines `font-regular|medium|semibold|bold` as family plus weight; `font-sans` is the regular file. Registry classes (`font-medium`) work unchanged; `font-light`, `font-thin`, `font-extrabold` and `font-black` do not exist (no file) and the class test fails on them. The kit's raw `Text` and `TextInput` got `font-regular` where they had no weight (a text with none has the system font). The names are `tokens.fontFamily.native`; `app.config.ts` derives the file list from it (a test checks the files exist). **Consequence:** the font files change the config, so the `runtimeVersion` fingerprint changes with the next build; an OTA update cannot reach a binary built before it (the next EAS build is a store build either way; owner list). The production-identity snapshot was regenerated for this one plugin entry.
- **2026-10-21 · Transform utilities are not used on native.** Tailwind's `rotate-*`, `scale-*` and `translate-*` set variables (`--tw-rotate`), and css-interop remounts a view that starts setting one after its first render; a pressed Accordion chevron looped the Jest renderer until it ran out of memory. The kit uses a `transform` style there (API.md says so).
- **2026-10-21 · The primitives are the hand-written native kit in Reusables' shape, not copies of Reusables.** The kit already had button, card, input, badge, tabs, dialog, alert-dialog, select, switch, checkbox, radio-group, progress, skeleton, avatar, toast, sheet, menu and stepper with web's names and props (phase 3), on plain React Native with NativeWind 4; Reusables' versions sit on `@rn-primitives/*` and a portal host and would change the exported API that Task 2 deliberately keeps (ruling 4). Added what it lacked, in the same style and with web's names: `Accordion`, `Alert`, `Label`, `Popover`, `Separator`, `Toggle` and `ToggleGroup`. The shadcn registry is unreachable from the sandbox (task 1), and `@rn-primitives` is not added (a dependency edge and a portal for no gained behaviour). Kept as before: the bottom sheet on `@gorhom`, the menu as an action sheet, the stepper. `Tooltip` stays web only (no hover); a long-press tooltip is a later decision if a screen needs one. `Popover` is a `Modal` anchored with `measureInWindow` (not verified on a device; API.md says so).
- **2026-10-21 · The kit's classes use the shadcn names where the value is equal.** `bg-card`, `bg-popover`, `text-muted-foreground`, `border-muted-foreground`, `bg-destructive`, `border-destructive` replace `bg-surface`, `bg-surface-raised`, `text-foreground-muted`, `bg-danger` and friends in `packages/ui-native` (values are equal by the alias test). `bg-surface-sunken`, `*-strong` and `*-subtle` stay: they have no shadcn name with the same value (`muted` differs from `surface-sunken` in dark mode). The app's screens still use the old names (Task 8 migrates them; Task 9 removes the aliases).
- **2026-10-21 · `Surface`: green surfaces are a kit primitive, applied to the screens by Task 8.** `Surface tone="primary" | "header"` provides the tone; `Text`, `Heading`, `Button` and `IconButton` read it: default and muted text become `primary-foreground` / `primary-foreground-muted` (or the `sidebar-*` pair), `Button`/`IconButton` primary become the orange `highlight` with `highlight-foreground` (green on green would vanish; its contrast pair already exists), `secondary` a white outline, `ghost` white; `danger` and the status text tones are unchanged (not AA on green). **Deviation: the native stack headers stay the page colour in this task.** Their action buttons (favorite, save to list, share, filter, menu) are rendered by screens Task 8 rewrites, and a green header over them now would put dark icons on green; Task 8 turns the headers green with `Surface` wrapped around the header actions. The splash is already the brand green, and the icons and the splash image are unchanged by Task 1 (the green did not move).
- **2026-10-21 · Parity with `@smog/ui-web` is a test.** `contract.test.tsx` holds the web kit's runtime exports at e690544 and fails when the native kit lacks one that is not in `WEB_ONLY` (with the reason), or exports a name that is neither shared, native only, nor in the Reusables list. A name Task 2 adds on the web is added to the lists in the change that gives it a native counterpart.
## Web primitives swap (phase 10a task 2)

- **2026-10-21 · Ruling 3, the components are hand-ported from a pinned registry checkout.** `ui.shadcn.com` answers 403 from the sandbox, so the CLI cannot generate (task 1). The source is `shadcn-ui/ui` at commit `ba1503c64472b82256d9de95e2e62c5901a38e8b` (`apps/v4/registry/bases/radix/ui/*.tsx`, style `radix-maia`). The procedure, to repeat on an update (a conscious step, never automatic): read the component, resolve each placeholder class (`cn-button`, `cn-card-header`, ...) to the `@apply` list of `apps/v4/registry/styles/style-maia.css` (markers with no rule, `cn-rtl-flip` and `cn-font-heading`, are dropped), replace the icon placeholders with `lucide-react`, rewrite the imports to `../lib/cn` and relative paths (the site's `@` alias points at the site's `src`, not the kit's), then edit for the kit's contracts below. Only the 37 components the kit uses were ported; no registry file is vendored unused (knip, tsc and Biome would object).
- **2026-10-21 · The registry's state variants live in `styles.css`.** The registry writes `data-open:`, `data-closed:`, `data-checked:`, `data-unchecked:`, `data-selected:`, `data-disabled:`, `data-active:`, `data-horizontal:` and `data-vertical:`; its CLI adds them as `@custom-variant`s (`packages/shadcn/src/tailwind.css`). They are copied into `@smog/ui-web/styles.css` (each matches Radix's `data-state` and the plain attribute), with the accordion and caret keyframes. The `tw-animate-css` classes (`animate-in`, `fade-in-0`, `zoom-in-95`, `slide-in-from-*`) are not carried: the kit keeps its own motion tokens (`animate-fade-in`, `animate-pop-in`, `animate-slide-*`), which are 0 ms under `prefers-reduced-motion`, and every component adds `motion-reduce:animate-none`.
- **2026-10-21 · Ruling 4, the public API is unchanged; the look is the registry's.** Every exported name and prop of the old kit stays (`Button` `primary | secondary | ghost | danger` is the registry's `default | outline | ghost | destructive`, `Select` takes `options`, `AlertDialog` takes `title`/`onConfirm`, ...), so screens compile untouched. What changed is the look (pill buttons and fields `rounded-4xl`, `rounded-2xl` cards and popovers, `ring-1 ring-foreground/10` instead of borders, `accent` fills on hovered menu rows) and the markup (`data-slot` on every root, Radix `data-state` styled through the variants above). The kit's old class tests were updated to the new vocabulary (`bg-destructive`, `border-input`, `data-checked:bg-primary`), not deleted.
- **2026-10-21 · The kit's contracts win where they meet the registry.** (1) Targets: the registry's `h-9` (36 px) controls become `min-h-touch` at `md` (44), `min-h-12` at `lg`, `sm` keeps 32 px plus the invisible 44 px `hitArea`; the same for Toggle, InputOTP slots, menu and command items, accordion triggers and sidebar buttons. (2) Focus: the registry's `ring-ring/50 ring-[3px]` is the kit's 2 px `focus-ring` ring with its offset (`focusRing`); inside `overflow-hidden` surfaces (accordion, scroll-area) it is `ring-inset`. (3) Hover: the registry's soft fills replace the old `stateLayer` on Button and pagination (`primary` hovers to `primary-strong`, since `primary/80` under white text would lose contrast); `stateLayer` stays on Chip, IconButton and SegmentedControl, which have no registry counterpart. (4) `danger` is a solid `destructive` fill with `destructive-foreground`, not the registry's 10 % tint with red text, which does not hold AA on every surface. (5) Fields use `border-input` (the 3:1 border token) on a `card` fill, not `bg-input/30`, because `input` is a strong grey here, not a wash. (6) Names come from `@smog/i18n`; the registry's English literals ("Toggle Sidebar", "More", "Previous slide") are keys (`a11y.toggleSidebar`, `a11y.more`, `a11y.previousSlide`, ...).
- **2026-10-21 · Toast is sonner, on `toast.custom`.** `ToastProvider` renders sonner's `<Toaster>` and `useToast()` returns a module-level `{ toast, dismiss }` (ids are ours, so `toast()` stays synchronous and the object is stable). Every toast is `toast.custom`: the kit owns the markup (variant icon and tone, one action, the dismiss button named `a11y.dismiss`), because sonner has no assertive option: a `danger` toast carries `role="alert"` inside sonner's polite `aria-live` section, which is named `a11y.notifications` through `customAriaLabel` (the default label appends a hard-coded English "alt+T" hint). Sonner injects its stylesheet with a `<style>` tag at import time; the CSP already allows `style-src 'unsafe-inline'`, so no header changes. The swipe, stack and hover pause are sonner's.
- **2026-10-21 · Sonner's store is global; the provider cleans up after itself, and the reduced-motion rule is completed.** Sonner keeps one module-wide queue and replays every active toast to a `Toaster` that mounts later, so a test (or a remounted root) saw the previous one's toasts (`maintenance` card test: "Maintenance is on" from the test before). `ToastProvider` therefore dismisses every toast when it unmounts (`toast.test.tsx` fails without it). Sonner switches its own motion off under `prefers-reduced-motion` for the toast and its children but not for the list that slides the stack (`ol[data-sonner-toaster]`, a 0.4 s transform), which `a11y-reflow` ("the toast does not move") caught; `styles.css` adds the missing `animation: none; transition: none` for the list.
- **2026-10-21 · `ScrollArea`'s viewport is a tab stop.** Radix's viewport is a scrolling `div` without `tabindex`, which axe reports (`scrollable-region-focusable`, serious) and which a keyboard user cannot scroll; the kit adds `tabIndex={0}` with the focus ring.
- **2026-10-21 · `Alert`, `Tabs` and the pill list keep the kit's tones.** `Alert` has `default | success | warning | danger` (the `*-strong` text on its `*-subtle` tint, the documented contrast pairs) and a role per tone (`danger` is `alert`, the rest `status`; the registry's always-`alert` interrupts a screen reader for a hint). `TabsList` gains `variant: "line" | "pill"`; `line` (the underline) stays the default because every current screen uses it, `pill` is the registry's muted track.
- **2026-10-21 · `command` and `carousel` are separate entry points; the rest is in the main barrel.** `@smog/ui-web/command` (cmdk) and `@smog/ui-web/carousel` (embla) are `exports` of the package, loaded with `lazy(() => import(...))` (ruling 5); the gallery does it. `@smog/ui-web` sets `"sideEffects": false`: without it the barrel's `export … from` lines keep every ported component in whatever chunk imports the barrel (measured: the registry gallery section alone added 30 kB gzip to the home entry, 482 kB against 452 kB; with the flag it adds 1.6 kB).
- **2026-10-21 · The sidebar is trimmed; the form is react-hook-form on top of `useFieldControl`.** `Sidebar` keeps the provider (`Ctrl/Cmd + B`), the fixed panel from `md` and a Sheet below it, the trigger, the inset, the header, content, footer, group and menu parts, and drops the rail, the sub-menus, the menu badge and action and the cookie that remembered the state (nothing uses them; the state is the caller's, `open` / `onOpenChange`). `Form` is the registry's react-hook-form form (`FormField`, `FormItem`, `FormLabel`, `FormControl`, `FormDescription`, `FormMessage`) whose `FormControl` passes `id`, `aria-describedby` and `aria-invalid` into the kit control, which merges them through `useFieldControl`; `FormMessage` is `role="alert"`. `useZodForm(schema, options)` is `useForm` with the zod resolver. `Field` (label, hint, error, counter without a form library) stays for the screens that do not need react-hook-form.
- **2026-10-21 · Hand-rolled stays where the registry has nothing.** `Stepper`, `Chip`, `SegmentedControl`, `OfflineBanner`, `EmptyState`, `ErrorState`, `Spinner`, `Logo`, `Text`, `Heading`, `TextLink`, `ListItem`, `SearchField`, `IconButton`, `DataTable` and the domain components keep their own code. The registry's `Empty`, `Item`, `Kbd`, `Spinner`, `ButtonGroup` and `InputGroup` were looked at and not ported: the kit's components already carry the contracts (i18n names, 44 px, tests) and the registry ones would only rename them.
- **2026-10-21 · [dep] Added to `@smog/ui-web`** (exact versions, newest stable at install, `registry` checked): `sonner` 2.0.8, `react-hook-form` 7.90.0, `@hookform/resolvers` 6.0.0 (zod 4 support), `input-otp` 1.5.0, `cmdk` 1.1.1, `embla-carousel-react` 8.6.0, and `zod` through the catalog. They are the registry components' own dependencies; `radix-ui` stays the unified package (ruling 6). No change to boundaries (they are third-party).
- **2026-10-21 · Bundle budgets re-measured.** Initial JS gzip, the entry that matters (`home`): 441,721 B on develop e690544, 470,047 B now (+28.3 kB, +6.4 %); the worker 1.88 MB to 2.00 MB (the gallery's new sections, dev and staging only). About 4 kB of it is sonner against the Radix toast, the rest the restyled components' longer class strings and the shared-chunk split the gallery's extra Radix pieces cause. The budgets in `perf-budget.json` (home 504,000) hold, so none was loosened; the headroom that remains is 7 % for home, which the next tasks (screens moving to registry blocks) will use, so each of them re-measures.
- **2026-10-21 · Tests: a failing `expect` on a DOM node is expensive.** Bun formats the whole happy-dom node (14 MB of log for one toast test, minutes of CPU). Polling helpers in the kit's tests throw a plain `Error` (`toast.test.tsx`), and a test that stubs a global (`command.test.tsx`: `ResizeObserver`, `scrollIntoView`) restores it in `afterAll`, because the files of one run share a process.

## Domain components on the registry parts (phase 10a task 3)

- **2026-10-21 · The cards, banners and states are rebuilt from registry parts and the brand, with their props unchanged (ruling 4).** `GestureCard` is video first: a `rounded-2xl` `card` surface, a large 3:4 still with a decorative play glyph (the brand green, `aria-hidden`, so the card still reads as a link to a gesture), the name in `text-body` semibold, and the categories as one `primary` Badge (`data-slot="gesture-category"`) instead of a grey line (the text is the same, `Greetings, Everyday`). The still zooms 5 % on hover and not under `prefers-reduced-motion`. `GestureRow` keeps its dense layout. `ConsentBanner` is a fixed region around a registry `Card` (raised, `max-w-content`), `CourseBanner` is an `Alert` (`warning`, the left rule kept; still a polite `status`, still named by its title), `OpenInAppBanner` sits on the `accent` tint (the quiet green), `AppBanner` stays on the green surface with pill store links and a brand hand (`primary-foreground` at 20 %, decorative, from `lg`), and `VideoPlayer` has a `rounded-2xl`, ringed, raised frame (`muted` behind the video and in the player's chrome). Tests were updated for the new classes and slots, none deleted.
- **2026-10-21 · `CategoryRef` gains an optional, web-only `icon`.** The catalogue has no category images yet (`categoryRefSchema` is name and slug), so "chips with image or icon" is a slot: a category that carries an `icon` (an `<img>` with dimensions, or a Lucide glyph) leads its chip with it. `Chip` already shows a check instead of the icon while selected, which keeps the state visible in more than colour. The schema is untouched; the screens that have artwork (Task 4's home) pass it. `ui-native` is not given the field (Task 8).
- **2026-10-21 · Reverses one Task 2 decision: the registry `empty` is ported.** Task 2 left `EmptyState` unrecomposed ("the registry `empty` was not ported"); the Task 3 brief asks for it. `Empty`, `EmptyHeader`, `EmptyMedia` (`default | icon`), `EmptyTitle` (a heading level, `text-title-1` at level 1, since the registry's `div` title has no level) , `EmptyDescription` and `EmptyContent` are in `@smog/ui-web`, hand-ported from the pinned commit like the rest (`bases/radix/ui/empty.tsx`, `cn-*` classes resolved from `style-maia.css`, the kit's type scale). `EmptyState` composes them and keeps its props (`illustration`, `icon`, `level`, `action`, the `states.empty.*` defaults); the `[data-slot=empty-illustration]` hook of its test stays. `ErrorState` is composed from the same parts, with a brand hand in `danger-strong` where the red icon disc was (still `role="alert"`).
- **2026-10-21 · The brand hands are a component, `HandIllustration`.** The three drawings of `@smog/brand/svg` (`currentColor`, decorative) were inlined by `EmptyState` alone. They are now one component (`hand` 0 to 2, colour by text class, size by height class) used by empty states, error states, the 404 (`EmptyState` level 1 with a hand, unchanged in the root route), the app banner and the auth side panel. The artwork is generated, trusted markup (`dangerouslySetInnerHTML`, as `Logo` does).
- **2026-10-21 · `AuthSidePanel` and the auth card.** The auth pages' card (`AuthCard`, shared by sign-in, sign-up, reset, verify and the magic-link pages) gains the green brand panel beside it from `md` (the reversed logo in `primary-foreground`, `home.tagline`, a hand at 30 %). It is decorative and hidden below `md`, so a phone shows the form alone (320 px reflow) and no landmark or heading is added (the card's `h1` stays the page's). Task 5 may rework the auth screens around it.
- **2026-10-21 · The global search is a lazy command palette; the header's search button and `/` open it.** `SearchPaletteProvider` (root layout; a context, the `/` shortcut) is a few hundred bytes of the main bundle; the palette (cmdk, the dialog, the search hooks) is its own chunk (`lazy`, imported the first time it opens). It shows the recent searches (empty query), the gestures ranked by the server (`useGestureSearch`, limit 6, `track: false`: `search_performed` measures the catalogue search and the palette's keystrokes would inflate it; the follow-up search page tracks as before), the categories that match (accent-insensitive, from `useCategories`) and a last item "Show all results for ...", which stores the query as a recent search and opens `/gestures?q=`. A gesture opens its page, a category `/gestures?category=`. cmdk does not filter (`shouldFilter={false}`; the list is the server's order), and its highlight is controlled and moved to the first item whenever the list changes, because cmdk keeps the old highlight when items change under it and Enter then opened "show all" instead of the gesture (found by the e2e). `/` opens it unless the focus is in a text field, a modifier is held, the key repeats or another dialog is open; the button has `aria-keyshortcuts="/"`. Escape closes it and returns the focus to the button (Radix). New copy: `searchPalette.{placeholder,description,seeAll}` in nl, en and fr; the rest reuses `common.search`, `nav.gestures`, `search.categories`, `search.recent` and `search.noResults.title`. Web only: the app's search is its own screen (Task 8).
- **2026-10-21 · Bundle.** Initial JS gzip of the home entry: 470,047 B (Task 2) to 471,845 B now (+1.8 kB); budget 504,000 unchanged. The palette, cmdk and the dialog stay out of it (the gallery and the palette load them on demand), and `sideEffects: false` is kept. The worker is unchanged in kind. No budget was touched.
- **2026-10-21 · Native contract list.** The web exports this task adds (`Empty*`, `HandIllustration`, `AuthSidePanel`) are entered in `WEB_ONLY` of `packages/ui-native/src/contract.test.tsx` with a reason; the native `EmptyState` keeps its own composition and `AuthSidePanel` is a web page decoration (the app's sign-in is Task 8's).

## Web shell, home, gestures and My space (phase 10a task 4)

- **2026-10-22 · The header is the green surface; one menu holds the user, Sponsor, language and theme.** The header is `bg-sidebar text-sidebar-foreground` (the deeper green `#006B50` of the sidebar roles, which `contrast.ts` already pairs with white text, the hover wash `sidebar-accent`, the border and the white `sidebar-ring`); the logo is the kit `Logo` in `currentColor` (white on the green, so no second SVG: the reversed lockup file is for the favicon and emails). Controls on it take the fragments of `app-shell/on-green.ts` (the registry ghost button and the kit icon button paint for the warm page). Inside: the logo, the two sections **Gestures** and **My space**, the search trigger (a pill with the `/` hint from `md`, an icon below it; both open the palette through `useSearchPalette().open`) and **one user and settings menu**. Signed in it is the avatar (account, admin for admins, sign out); as a guest it is a settings icon (sign in) with a "Sign in" button beside it from `md`. Both menus end with **Sponsor**, the language radios and the theme radios (`LanguageMenuItems`, `ThemeMenuItems`; `LanguageControl` and `ThemeControl` stay for the account preferences). The phone's sheet now holds only the sections (language and theme moved into the menu), so a phone has no sign-in button in the header: it is the first item of the settings menu (the former three-band label/icon rule for the sign-in link at 320 to 420 px is gone, and the reflow spec pins the new header at 390 and 320). The menu scrolls inside the viewport (`--radix-dropdown-menu-content-available-height`). Alternative: keep the language and theme icon buttons (two more 44 px targets on a green bar that already has to fit 320 px).
- **2026-10-22 · Sponsor moves to the footer and the menus.** The footer is four columns on `md` (the logo and the tagline, Explore, Support SMOG, Legal), links with a 44 px target; Sponsor is in "Support SMOG" with one line of why and in both header menus. The footer's group names are labelled navigation landmarks, not headings (a heading per column made `heading-order` depend on what the page above ends with).
- **2026-10-22 · `/my` is one route with tabs; `/favorites` and `/lists` answer 302 from the Worker.** `routes/my.tsx` has `?tab=favorites|lists` (Favorites when absent) and, on Lists, `?id=` (the open list); anything else is dropped by `validateMySearch`. The two old screens became `FavoritesPanel` and `ListsPanel` (same hooks, states and dnd-kit behaviour, the guest's device store or the account, as before); Task 5 reworks the screens and adds "Shared by me". `/favorites` and `/lists` are rows of `LEGACY_REDIRECTS` with the new `search` (the parameters the new page needs, placed first, the old query kept after them) and `status: 302` fields: **302, not 301**, because this is a product decision this phase may revise, not a URL the old site used (a 301 would be cached for ever, see the token links); `/lists/<shareToken>` is a different path and is untouched. The Worker answers them before Start, so the redirect also works for crawlers and a no-JS browser, and `routes-parity.spec.ts` (HTTP only) pins status and `Location`. The `lists` entry of `perf-budget.json` is now `my` (`my.tsx` and `lists/$shareToken.tsx`).
- **2026-10-22 · "Continue where you left off" is the device's recent gestures, in the local store.** `preferences.recentGestureIds` (optional and additive like `appBannerDismissedAt`: stored v2 data still parses, no migration; at most `RECENT_GESTURES_MAX` = 6), the mutators `addRecentGesture` (newest first, deduped, a repeat of the front is no write) and `clearRecentGestures`, and in `@smog/gestures/client` `useRememberGesture(id)` (the gesture page and the detail beside the results call it) and `useRecentGestures()` (the ids from the store, the gestures from the public `gestures.byIds`, unpublished ones skipped). Like recent searches the ids never leave the device and need no consent (nothing is sent but the public read). Home shows the section only when there is something to continue, with a "Clear" button, a skeleton while the gestures load and nothing (and a log line) when the read fails: it is a convenience, the featured gestures below are the page. The app does not use it yet (Task 8 may).
- **2026-10-22 · Home: a green hero, illustrated category chips, one banner slot last.** The hero is `bg-primary` (the filled green, white text; the brand hand at `lg`) with the title, the tagline and the big search (`SearchBox`, unchanged, with its recent searches). The categories are links with a brand hand in a tinted circle (the three hands in turn: the schema has no category image, `CategoryRef.icon` stays an unused slot); the featured gestures keep their loader prefetch. The app banner (`SiteAppBanner`) moved from under the hero to the end of the page: it and the recent gestures mount after hydration, and what mounts late must sit under what is seen first (review M6; a returning visitor's recents still push the featured gestures down, which is below the fold on a phone but not on a tall desktop).
- **2026-10-22 · Filters in a sheet on phones; the detail's actions in view.** `CategoryFilterTrigger` (a "Filters" button with the number chosen, a bottom sheet with the same `CategoryChips`, applying as they are picked, "Show results" closing it) is `md:hidden`; `CategoryFilterInline` is the chips under the search field from `md`. The master-detail (rows beside the detail from `lg`) is kept. The gesture page gives the video the larger column (6fr against 5fr, up to 32 rem on a phone) and shows favorite, save, **Share** and **QR code** as buttons instead of the overflow menu (nothing is hidden behind "Acties").
- **2026-10-22 · Tests.** The ring check of `a11y-keyboard.spec.ts` takes the token: the header's controls ring in `sidebar-ring`, everything else in `focus-ring`. The language and theme menu tests became the settings-menu tests; the guest routes of the a11y and reflow specs list `/my?tab=favorites` and `/my?tab=lists` (the titles spec lists `/my` once: both tabs share a title); `home.spec.ts` and `my-space.spec.ts` are new (public project). New copy lives in the blocks `shell`, `mySpace`, `landing` and `browse` of the three catalogues.

## Web screens: My space, account, auth, sponsor, legal, errors (phase 10a task 5)

- **2026-10-22 · "Shared by me" is a tab of My space, built on `ListSummary.shares`.** `?tab=shared` lists the account's lists that have an active view or edit link (a badge per kind, the item count), each opening in the Lists tab where the share sheet already renews or revokes. No new procedure: `lists.list` already says per list whether a view or edit link exists. A guest sees an empty state that asks them to sign in (sharing needs an account, spec §11) and the sign-in link comes back to `/my?tab=shared`. The lists tab keeps dnd-kit reorder, the share sheet and the QR dialog unchanged.
